戰火影響料屬短期，無礙市場中期走勢
2026 年 4 月
美國對伊朗展開襲擊所引發的中東戰火延續至今，拖累 3 月截至 27 日的全球金融股市表現，
市場在多重變數交織下動能顯著受壓，主要股市指數單月報酬全盤皆墨。不過，在能源類股大漲的支
撐下，S&P 500 指數表現相對其他股市穩定。日本股市雖亦出現獲利了結壓力，但受惠於高市政府
「負責且積極的財政政策」 及日圓匯率弱勢有利出口 ， 今年以來 MSCI 日本指數的表現僅次於拉丁美
洲股市。另一方面，受油價高漲帶動通膨預期、外資持續撤出且經濟放緩所累，印度股市出現賣壓，

MSCI 印度指數不論在 3 月或是今年至今表現都是敬陪末座。
全球主要債市 3 月報酬亦全數下跌。在所有券種之中 ， 以新興當地債下跌 5.21% 表現最為疲弱 ，
除了油價引發市場對於進口原油的新興國家前景的擔憂外 ，高漲的美元指數也使得當地貨幣計價新興
債表現呈壓。 全球主權債同樣因為油價高漲推升通膨疑慮 ，投資人對於各國央行貨幣政策或因而轉為
更為鷹派高度警戒，使其表現差強人意。
自去年川普關稅「解放日」後美股跌至低點後開始走揚，各國股市與主要指數持續上揚，其中又
以金礦指數、南韓股市、費半指數、日本股市表現最佳。然而，自中東戰事 2/28 爆發後，以指數修
正幅度來看，跌幅最大的市場分別為金礦指數、南韓股市、日本股市。唯有費半指數則在 AI 需求暢
旺的加持下成為例外。由此可見，本波市場的回跌，基本上為「漲多跌多」的修正效應。
就基本面來看，2026 年美股企業獲利增長主軸仍在 AI， 由於2026 年仍在 AI 基礎建設期擴張間 ，
AI 終端需求仍是科技龍頭企業，故所帶動的獲利增長料不致受油價影響，今年單季盈餘增長可望維

持 2 位數水準。自中東戰事以來，彭博對於 MSCI 美國指數 Q1 及 Q2 的 EPS 預期不跌反漲，顯示市
場對於美國企業的獲利前景仍極具信心。同時，目前的資訊科技類股的預期 P/E 比與過去 10 年的均
值相較仍過有遭到低估之嫌，也預示科技類股相較於其他類股仍有進一步向上的動能。
再者，儘管近期美國公債殖利率因市場的通膨疑慮而上揚 ， 但目前殖利率遠高於Fed 長期通膨目
標，投資人仍可以資產配置角度對於債券作適當之配置。考量短期殖利率受市場對 Fed 的利率政策
預期變動影響較大，故在年期與券種的選擇上可以 7~15 年的中長期美國公債或投資級債為主。

美國股市：標普 500 企業盈餘增長加速，私募信貸贖回潮不致衝擊美國經濟
美國第四季 GDP 季增年率修正值 0.7%，較初值 1.4%有所調降，主要反映實質個人消費季比降
至 0.7%。第四季 GDP 數據整體仍支持美國經濟穩健。但第一季受通膨(關稅與伊朗危機)與高利率影
響，消費行為呈現「降級消費」特徵，非必需性支出收縮，經濟呈現倚賴「高收入族群支撐消費」的
K 型分化格局。由於中東戰火推高能源價格，美國民眾擔心生活成本壓力惡化，美國密西根大學3 月
消費者信心指數降至 55.5，較 2 月終值 56.6 下降 1.1，觸及今年以來最低紀錄，反映中東危機與生

活成本壓力削弱民眾樂觀情緒。
2 月非農就業人數淨減少 9.2 萬個，低於彭博預期的增加 1.3 萬，失業率躍升至 4.4%，失業率雖
短期上揚、但就長期角度仍在低檔。追究原因數據主要是受到罷工和天氣等短期因素干擾，導致醫療
健保、建築業、零售業和休閒酒店等產業表現不佳，但整體勞動市場狀態溫和，從請領就業金人數等
數據看來就業市場尚未出現顯著惡化跡象。市場先前擔憂 AI 取代人力，目前數據看來企業僅是招募
保守，後續可觀察勞動市場降溫是否反映在薪資成長，若薪資壓力下降有助於抑制服務業通膨，除直
接改善企業成本結構外，也將使聯準會擔憂的服務業通膨出現放緩。
預估第一季標普 500 企業餘年增仍可達到 11.3%，且在第二季顯著拉升至近兩成水準，科技板
塊受惠於 AI 仍是帶動美股企業獲利增長主軸。大型科技巨擘透過AI 優化流程，開始對利潤率產生正
面影響。然而，此一效益目前高度集中於資本與技術優勢明顯的科技龍頭企業，中小企業與非科技產
業尚未顯著受益。因此，企業獲利將呈現明顯分化格局，而尚未受惠於 AI 的非科技業者，將會在未

來幾年逐步提高使用 AI 程度，傳統企業大規模導入 AI 模型與使用算力，將可成為 AI 龍頭下一波業
績成長動能。
近期美國私募信貸市場爆發贖回潮，包含貝萊德、阿波羅、藍鷹(Blue Owl)等巨頭均遭投資人要
求提款，甚至限制贖回以防止流動性枯竭。此波動盪主因投資人擔憂 AI 技術衝擊軟體業導致違約風
險上升，以及對高收益資產的獲利了結。研判此類事件雖造成金融股拉回，但引發總體系統性危機的
可能性極低。
目前標普 500 本益比估計在 22.84 倍、股價淨值比 4.95 倍，短期評價偏高，面對伊朗戰事的干
擾，預料股市震盪加劇，但若市場拉回，基於美國在 AI 產業具備絕對優勢，仍建議以長線角度布局。

歐洲股市：歐元區經濟表現穩健，央行面對能源危機採取警戒態度
歐元區第四季 GDP 季增率自初值的 0.3%下修至 0.2%，年增則下修至 1.2%，二者皆低於市場預
期。歐元區第四季經濟動能略微放緩，成長主要由內需帶動。家庭消費在穩健的勞動力市場與薪資增
長支撐下，季增率上升至 0.2%，固定資本形成雖由高點回落，但仍維持正成長，外部需求疲軟導致
出口季減，加上企業去庫存，顯示內需支撐正與貿易逆風強烈拉鋸。
三月份歐元區經濟活動連續 15 個月處於擴張區間，其中製造業表現強勁，歐元區製造業PMI 由
50.8 上升至 51.4，創 45 個月高點，受新訂單增長支撐，惟出口訂單連續第 49 個月低於榮枯線，外

需疲軟仍未改善。服務業受中東戰爭引發的不確定性與能源成本飆升衝擊，驟降至 50.1，放緩至 10
個月低點。預料南歐以觀光業為主國家將面對能源成本衝擊，而外銷為主的如德國、法國將迎來能源
與運輸成本飆升的雙重打擊。歐盟決定延後提交原定 4/15 提出將俄羅斯石油進口永久禁令入法的提
案，自 2022 年俄羅斯全面入侵烏克蘭以來，歐盟已大幅降低對俄能源依賴，是否因為伊朗戰爭調整
制裁內容將是未來關注重點。
歐洲央行在 3 月利率決策會議決定利率不變、但態度轉鷹。歐洲央行總裁拉加德表示，若通膨出
現「顯著但不過於持久」的超標情況，可能進行「適度的政策調整」，以確保通膨預期維持穩定。她
強調 ， 若對通膨偏離目標完全不作反應 ， 恐影響政策溝通與市場信心 ， 使外界難以理解央行反應機制 。
從利率期貨觀察 ， 市場預期歐洲央行與英國央行都有機會在第二季升息1 碼 ， 不排除未來一年升息到
2 碼，顯示歐洲央行與英國央行面對能源危機採取警戒態度。
在地緣風險常態化背景下，歐洲各國持續提高國防支出，德國政府規劃在2026 年投入 380 億歐

元於基礎建設、氣候保護專項基金、國防及數位化，加上 AI 浪潮帶動企業投資，投資者原本對歐洲
經濟抱持樂觀態度，然而油價大漲大幅扭轉市場樂觀預期。 考量市場目前悲觀原因仍是地緣政治風
險，假設美伊衝突能在第二季落幕，對歐股企業獲利影響有限，歐洲經濟將在下半年重回成長軌道。
泛歐 600 指數目前本益比為 16.14，故價淨值比為 2.27。就評價角度相對合理，雖然年初以來歐
股的樂觀情緒因為伊朗戰爭快速扭轉，但未來若油價回落，或是歐洲重新考慮向俄羅斯購買能源，都
能夠化解歐洲面臨的能源困境，故仍可視為長線佈局標的。

日本股市：高市政府加大戰略產業投資，政府補貼壓抑油價衝擊
日本受惠於企業強勁支出 ，2025 年第四季 GDP 年化季增率為 1.3%， 優於原先預估的0.2%。2026
年不僅企業資本支出動能持續 ， 在高市早苗出任首相後 ， 政府選定支持包含實體人工智慧 、 再生醫學 、
量子運算與海洋無人機等 61 個項目，目標在 2040 年前取得全球 AI 機器人逾 30%的市占率，國產
晶片銷售額也要在同年衝上 40 兆日圓。高市希望藉由政府與民間的合作推動投資，強化發展包含人
工智慧、半導體、宇宙及航太等 17 大重點產業。由於自民黨已在國會選舉取得空前壓倒性勝利，產

業補貼計畫將可落實，讓政府投資不僅貢獻短期 GDP，亦可提升日本長期經濟競爭力。
日本 2 月剔除生鮮食品的消費者價格年比上漲 1.6%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最小升幅，主要因為
公用事業補貼壓低能源成本。日本勞動組合總連合會(連合)近期公布 2026 年春鬥第 1 次彙整結果，
加薪幅度雖低於去年同期 、 不過已連3 年高於 5%，且2026 年中小企業加薪幅度可望高於過去幾年，
支撐日本薪資物價形成正向循環，並達成實質薪資增長。近期伊朗戰事導致油價飆升，首相高市早苗
積極應對，除釋出戰備儲油外，將控制汽油價格上限，故預料短期通膨不致大幅躍昇。
日本央行三月利率決策會議維持政策不變，主要考量來自中東局勢不確定性，難以衡量伊朗戰爭
及國際油價飆漲對日本經濟及通膨帶來影響 ，呼應日本央行總裁植田和男論點 ， 希望有更多時間可以
觀察對物價的引導。後市央行升息時點須留意4 月初將公布的短觀報告，若企業信心尚維持正面，搭
配伊朗戰爭如白宮所預期的在 5、6 周之內結束，則日本央行或有望在第二季升息一碼。預料升息進

度放緩將使十年期日本公債殖利率在 2.0~2.5%區間震盪，且由於全球央行方向轉鷹、美元受惠於避
險需求，日本央行緩步升息有助日圓匯率穩定。
波克夏將對東京海上進行出資 ， 在4 月藉由收購東京海上持有的自家庫藏股 、 取得東京海上2.49%
股權，出資額為 2,874 億日圓。在巴菲特掌舵時期，波克夏對日本企業的投資以大型商社為主，此次
將是波克夏首度和日本金融機構進行合作 ， 性質與過去投資商社股不同 ， 若波克夏未來對日投資可擴
及其他非商社股，對於日股長期表現將帶來更大力度的加持。
2026 年以來日股受惠於高市擴大財政刺激 、強化軍事安全、鞏固美日同盟上，但本益比仍在 18.38
倍，股價淨值比在 1.68 倍。3 月下旬外資賣超日股，估計是受到伊朗戰事干擾且避免股利發放的課
稅而調整持股部位所致。近期日圓震盪走貶，有利於於日本外銷股財報表現，預料企業獲利可望為日
股在全球金融市場震盪下帶來支撐。

新興亞洲股市：中東戰事對經濟影響負面，但成長仍具韌性
亞洲股市 3 月因中東情勢緊張而大跌，截至 3/27，亞洲不含日本指數 (MXAPJ) 單月下跌
10.39%。南韓股市跌幅居前，單月下跌 12.90%，多次於盤中啟動熔斷機制，除了中東地緣政治風
險之外，漲幅過大後的技術性修正也是主因。台股在內資支撐下，跌幅相對較輕，單月下跌 6.50%。
中國股市也表現弱勢，上證指數下跌 5.99%，國企指數下跌 4.58%。3 月份正值全國兩會結束，股市
出現了「利多出盡」的調節。MSCI 東協指數 3 月下跌 3.53%，是跌幅較輕的亞洲區域。馬來西亞與

印尼身為原油、液化天然氣（LNG）及棕櫚油的重要出口國，油價上漲直接帶動其相關權值股走強，
抵銷了跌幅，但越南、菲律賓等原油進口國，跌幅超過 10%。印度 SENSEX 指數下跌 9.48%，印度
是全球第三大石油進口國，約 85% 的原油依賴進口，油價高漲對經濟造衝擊，另外外資於三月大量
賣出，也是印度股市重挫的原因。
亞洲經濟在 2026 年 Q2 將面對能源價格的劇烈波動對經濟的衝擊。根據 ADB 資料，泰國、
南韓與新加坡及印度等國，其能源進口依賴度顯著高於區域平均 。在當前中東衝突陰影未散的背景
下，油價的每一次跳動都觸發亞洲通膨的扳機，對於這些原油高依賴進口國家而言，能源成本的上升
不僅僅是物價問題，更是對 GDP 增長動能的直接侵蝕。
根據野村的推估「油價每上漲 10%」對各國經濟的衝擊路徑各不相同，印度與菲律賓的 CPI 通
膨壓力最為顯著，預計分別跳升 0.5 個百分點，這將迫使兩國央行在 Q2 維持較高利率環境，壓縮
降息空間 。反觀馬來西亞與印尼，受惠於其資源生產國的地位，GDP 增長甚至可能出現微幅的正向

回饋或較小的負面衝擊 。這種「能源非對稱性」將導致亞洲內部經濟與貨幣政策的進一步分化。
然而亞洲經濟在 2026 年 Q2 面臨增長放緩的挑戰。但根據博鰲亞洲論壇報告表示，受國際貿易不
確定性及地緣政治風險上升影響，今年亞洲增長率預計從去年的 4.7% 降至 4.5%。儘管增速微降，
但亞洲經濟體 GDP 占世界比重持續上升，依然是全球經濟的主要成長引擎。這與 ADB 資料相呼
應，顯示亞洲在全球經濟增速放緩的大環境下，憑藉區域一體化進程展現出較佳的韌性 。
對於印度與菲律賓等高通膨敏感區，應關注其內需韌性能否抵銷能源稅的負面影響；而對於新加
坡、南韓與台灣等雖然進口佔比較高但產業鏈地位穩固的經濟體，仍看好在全球 AI 供應鏈帶來的快
速增長。整體而言亞洲國家在 2026 年的經濟增長整體保持韌性，但將出現明確分化。

中國股市：轉型陣痛與外部地緣風險的夾擊
2026 年中國兩會不僅發佈經濟目標，更是「十五五規劃」首年的轉型宣言。官方將 GDP 成長
目標設定在 4.5% 至 5.0% 的區間，這項數據釋放了一個明確信號，北京已不再盲目追求過往的高
速增長，而是將重心置於「新質生產力」的質變與落地。在政策宣示中，我們觀察到由「總量擴張」
向 「結構優化」 的顯著移轉。2026 年數位經濟核心產業佔 GDP 比重目標提升至 12.5%，重點布局
人工智能（AI Agent）與具身智能（Physical AI）的工業化應用。同時，政府推動的大規模「設備更

新與消費品以舊換新」 政策，試圖在傳統房產動能消退之際，透過激發存量市場的替代需求來維持內
需熱度。這種以新經濟與高品質消費為主的政策組合，旨在對沖房地產下行對內需產生的結構性缺
口，並賦予國家經濟轉型的重任。
中國經濟正經歷一場歷史性的動能切換，長期作為增長引擎的房地產，其最終需求對 GDP 的佔
比正持續回落。與此同時，以醫藥、先進設備、IT 通訊及研發為首的「高科技 + 新三樣」產業，其
貢獻權重已經在 2025 年正式超越房地產。轉型過程雖然伴隨著陣痛，卻是中國邁向科技自主的必要
路徑 。 儘管房地產的長尾效應仍在消化 ， 但這些新興領域的快速擴張正為中國經濟建立更具韌性的基
礎，成為觀察中國能否維持區域增速預期的核心支柱。對於投資者而言，這意味著舊有的投資邏輯已
徹底改變，未來市場的超額收益將高度集中在具備全球競爭力的先進製造龍頭。
儘管新經濟轉型積極，但房地產市場的下滑壓力依然是中國最沉重的負擔。根據彭博模型，房地
產尋底之路極為漫長。預測路徑顯示，未來幾年房地產佔 GDP 比重將持續收縮至 12% 左右。中

國的政策博弈重點在於政府如何透過擴張性財政手段來進行 「止血」 ，防止房市超跌引發系統性金融
風險。但在目前市場信心修復極為緩慢的背景下，觀察銷售數據能否在超跌區間出現初步止穩。
然而中國面臨的外部環境更加嚴峻。中東危機的突然升溫，成為干擾復甦的關鍵變數。地緣震盪
推升了能源溢價，加劇輸入型通膨壓力，美國聯準會（Fed）態度的轉向鷹派與美元持續走強，顯著
壓縮了中國央行的降息空間。在強美元與高利差的雙重擠壓下，人民幣匯率面臨巨大的貶值壓力。這
種「內需待援、外部受限」的政策困局，使得操作空間顯得倍加受限，雖然兩會釋放了擴張性財政的
積極信號，但在地緣政治風險與強勢美元背景下，市場缺乏強勁的資金流入動能，也因此我們認為中
國股市欠缺全面性上漲的機會。類股建議在配置上規避傳統房地產產業，鎖定受惠於新質生產力政
策、具備技術護城河且能抵禦外部波動的科技領先標的。

印度股市： 中東戰事與 Fed 轉鷹影響印度成長動能
印度的實體經濟活動成長發出趨緩信號。3 月份最新發布的 HSBC Flash 印度綜合 PMI 指數由 2
月的 58.9 降至 56.5，創下 2022 年 10 月以來最弱的擴張速度，反映出國內需求在物價壓力下正
逐步降溫 。其中，製造業 PMI 降至 53.8，顯示國內訂單成長受阻；然而，值得關注的是國際訂單
卻創下調查歷史新高，這證明在全球供應鏈重組的戰略大環境下，印度製造的外部需求依然堅挺 。
儘管基本面仍具韌性，但 PMI 所揭示的投入成本增速創多年新高，預示著企業利潤空間將面臨嚴峻

的輸入型通膨擠壓，這將是觀察印度經濟能否維持高成長的首要指標。
儘管短期數據出現波動，但印度作為全球增長引擎的核心地位並未動搖。在 2026-27 財政預算
案的架構下， 政府持續推動以基礎設施投資為核心的擴張性政策 ， 試圖透過提升公共支出效率來強化
製造業基礎。印度正積極利用其人口紅利與政策激勵，吸引從電子組裝到半導體測試的全球業者進
駐。然而，這種高度依賴資本流入與外部能源供給的增長模式，在地緣政治動盪與聯準會轉向鷹派的
環境下，正迎來一場壓力測試。「內部政策利多」與「外部環境利空」的互相干擾，使得印度的成長
前景短期面臨不確定性 。
能源價格震盪是印度經濟短期最重要的潛在風險。根據彭博經濟的報導，印度央行估計，原油價
格每上漲 10 美元，經濟成長將會下降 0.15%。若中東衝突導致霍爾木茲海峽關閉三個月，2027 財
年成長率恐重挫至 6.1% 。在極端情況下，燃料成本飆升將引發連鎖反應，不僅削弱消費者的實際收
入，更會因國營石油公司虧損削減股利，進而擠壓預算案中的公共建設支出與政府投資能力。除了價

格波動，供應端的物理性中斷亦是威脅。印度的能源約 85%依賴進口，若航運中斷導致供應缺口，
包含民生消費與石化產業等經濟活動都將受到供應鍊斷裂的風險。
在貨幣政策上 ， 印度央行面臨 「內外夾擊」 。 若油價衝向140 美元 ，CPI 預計將進一步升 5.0%，
並可能在 9 月突破 RBI 的 6%容忍區間，央行被迫在年底臨時升息 2 碼。外部方面，聯準會態度的轉
向鷹派加劇了資金回流美元資產的壓力，也將干擾印度央行的寬鬆節奏。綜合上述分析，我們對印度
市場看法轉向中性。雖然結構性紅利與財政擴張確保了其增長基本盤 ， 但近期的重心已由 「追求擴張」
轉向「防禦通膨與能源衝擊」，這讓 PMI 顯示內需趨緩。加上潛在的油價的大漲與供應減少，正大
幅抵銷供應鏈轉移的長期利多。我們建議在股市配置上採取守勢。在近期的估值修正期，利用市場震
盪，重新評估基本面。

拉美股市：巴西正式啟動降息循環，中東衝突有利拉美經濟
巴西央行貨幣政策委員會(Copom)於 3 月 18 日會議全票通過將基準利率調降 25 個基點至
14.75%，正式啟動新一輪降息循環。Copom 會議紀要強調該決定與當前高度不確定性的背景相符，
在地緣政治衝突持續時間與範圍難以預判 、 經濟活動放緩步調及其對物價水準影響的訊號錯綜複雜之
下，保持從容並收集額外資訊為適當之舉。Copom 也為下次會議將降息步調加速至 50 個基點保
留空間，但也強調需美伊戰爭等外部環境改善且這些事件對通膨與通膨預期影響有限才能達成。

市場通膨預期方面，截至 3 月 30 日當周巴西央行市場調查顯示，2026 年通膨預期上升 14
個基點至 4.31%，高於巴西央行 3.0% 的長期通膨目標中點，2027、2028 及 2029 年則分別為
3.84%、3.57% 及 3.50%，惟有鑑於近期油價持續走升， 預計未來數週通膨預期將可能進一步惡化。
此外，市場對 2026 年底 Selic 利率中位預期維持在 12.50%，2027 及 2028 年底分別為 10.50%
及 10.00%，顯示雖中東戰爭帶動市場通膨升溫，然而巴西市場整體降息格局並未受到太大影響，仍
是朝大幅降息的方向前進。
另一方面，雖美伊戰爭帶動短線油價上漲，帶動拉美市場通膨預期，然而油價上漲事實上也有利
於拉美整體經濟，主因拉美市場中，包含阿根廷、巴西、哥倫比亞與厄瓜多等國為石油淨出口國，高
盛研究指出若油價上漲 10%，能使整體拉美市場未來 12 個月 GDP 上升約 0.2%，顯示美伊戰爭
對拉美市場來說並非全然只有負面影響。
最後一個值得關注的議題是巴西 2026 年的總統大選，市場主要關注現任總統盧拉，與前總統

波索納洛之子弗拉維奧的對決。市調機構 Quaest 3 月民調結果顯示，在第二輪決選中，兩位候選人
首度打成平手，各獲得 41% 支持度，扭轉過往皆由盧拉勝出的局勢，顯示中東衝突引發的能源價格
上漲與通膨壓力持續影響盧拉政權的支持度 。市場預期若最終總統大選由右派陣營的弗拉維奧勝出，
其政策將會有利於巴西財政赤字問題改善，相較於盧拉政營財政擴張的策略 ，更可能吸引外資流入巴
西市場。
展望後市 ，儘管中東衝突帶動拉美市場通膨預期攀升 ，然而拉美市場最大經濟體巴西仍處於降息
循環中，且目前市場並不預期巴西央行會因為能源價格問題，而扭轉寬鬆方向，此外，拉美市場整體
亦會受惠於油價上漲帶動的石油出口助益，故整體而言，我們目前對拉美市場後市仍抱持正向看法。

能源類股：美伊停戰協商短期難有結論，可以資產配置角度布局能源類股
美伊衝突仍是 3 月主導能源價格走勢的關鍵，雖然美國在 3 月 25 日透過巴基斯坦向伊朗提出停
戰協議，然而內容與伊朗訴求相差甚遠，預料短期內美伊達成停戰協議的可能性並不高，戰事仍可能
需要來回數次的武力攻擊與談判後，才能底定。
隨戰爭持續，荷莫茲海峽的中斷程度仍是油價最關鍵的變數，戰前荷莫茲海峽每天約有 130 艘
船通過海峽 ， 目前每天僅有不到 6 艘在伊朗許可下通行，海峽運輸仍幾乎處於癱瘓狀態 。 對伊朗而言 ，
封鎖荷莫茲海峽越久 ， 越能為其換得更好的談判籌碼 ， 伊朗國會目前更是在推動立法對通過海峽的船

舶收取過路費，以此將海峽視為經濟收入來源，故何時荷莫茲海峽能夠暢通，仍具高度不確定性。
受荷莫茲海峽運量持續受阻影響，高盛 3 月 23 日報告再度上修油價預測，預估 3 月布蘭特原油
平均價格將達 105 美元，4 月將進一步上升至 115 美元，並在 2026 年第四季回落至 80 美元，該預
測基於荷莫茲海峽石油流量將在 6 週內維持極低水準的假設。此外，在海峽流量中斷 10 週的不利情
境下，高盛預估布蘭特油價將可能接近或超越 2008 年 147 美元的歷史紀錄，隨後在2026 年第四季
放緩至 100 美元；在中斷10 週並同時伴隨基礎設施受損的嚴重不利情境下，油價可能飆至接近160
美元，並在2026 年第四季仍維持 115 美元高油價。在高盛各種情境分析中，若海峽流量中斷時間延
長，或者有更多能源基礎設施受損，長期油價都存在上升的可能性，有利能源類股表現。
另一方面，觀察 Bloomberg 西德州原油期貨遠期曲線，可以看出目前市場仍定價荷莫茲海峽受
阻為中短期事件，曲線在開戰後前端快速上揚，反映現貨端供給緊張狀況，然而遠端合約變動幅度不

大，且近幾個月合約曲線陡峭，顯示市場認為荷莫茲海峽受阻仍將是短期事件，並不預期戰爭將會演
變成長期戰事，預料隨衝突結束，原油供給將大幅恢復，帶動油價下跌。3 月 30 日 WTI 遠期曲線顯
示，油價將會在 11 月回落至每桶 80 美元以下。此外，截至 3/30，國際賭盤 Polymarket 預估美伊
在 4/15 前停戰機率約 17%，4/30 前為 32%，6/30 前為 59%，12/31 前則為 75%， 機率在 4/30
至 6/30 區間增幅最大，隱含市場對停火的預期主要落在 4~6 月。
展望後市，雖然近期美伊皆提出談判條件，然而雙方在談判條件上仍存在顯著落差，再加上對於
伊朗來說，將荷莫茲海峽封鎖的越久，讓能源價格維持高檔，越能為伊朗得到更好的談判籌碼，故預
估伊朗更可能採取邊打邊談的策略，將整體談判時程拉長。對於能源類股來說，短線上若戰爭持續，
能源類股有望走出獨立行情，並可作為投資組合中對衝戰爭風險的角色，而即使在停戰後，長期油價
仍有望高於戰前水準，亦有利支撐能源類股表現，故建議可以資產配置角度，適度布局能源類股。

黃金類股：美伊戰事壓抑短線金價表現，惟反轉條件已逐步成形
黃金傳統上被視為戰爭期間的避險資產，然而本波自 2 月 28 日美伊戰爭爆發以來，金價卻明顯
表現不佳，從戰前約 5,100 美元一路下跌，一度回撤至約 4,100 美元位置。本波金價走疲，主因美伊
戰爭導致荷莫茲海峽受阻，持續推升油價，帶動市場通膨預期上升，同步推高了美國公債殖利率，使
不孳息的黃金資產吸引力下降。此外，市場避險情緒亦帶動美元走強，而黃金價格長期以來與美元走
勢呈負相關，隨美元指數走升，黃金等以美元計價之商品價格亦出現承壓。
此外，黃金重要買盤全球央行，近期購金動能也出現明顯放緩。世界黃金協會報告指出，2026

年 1 月全球央行淨購金量驟降至 5 噸，較去年月均購金量 27 噸銳減近 80%，推測主因 1 月金價波
動過大，導致各國央行態度轉為觀望。 另一方面，土耳其央行近期大幅減持黃金 ，也對市場情緒造
成壓抑。據彭博新聞引述知情人士報導，土耳其央行在 3 月 13 日至 3 月 20 日的兩周內，以出售及
協議交換置換了約 60 噸黃金，用以穩定因美伊戰爭而受影響的土耳其里拉匯率，彭博指出，土耳其
央行本次減持黃金，一部分是採市場直接拋售，一部分則是通過 SWAP 以黃金作為抵押品來獲取外
匯或里拉 ，而歷史上各國央行透過 SWAP 以貴金屬獲得廉價美元融資的情況並不少見，且回顧 2023
年 3 月至 5 月期間，土耳其央行亦曾售出近 160 噸黃金，惟當時因來自黃金 ETF 的買家吸收賣壓，
對短線金價並未造成太大影響，故若僅有來自土耳其央行的一次性賣壓，實際並不需要太過擔憂。
更需要留意的是 ， 中東戰爭將可能迫使其他國家同樣採取出售黃金儲備的動作，以支撐外匯存底
或籌措資金，若此趨勢擴大，將 可能進一步增加黃金短線賣壓， 同時，因目前市場資金較不偏好黃

金，短線上黃金 ETF 吸納來自各國央行賣壓的力道也會較弱，故只要美伊戰爭持續，黃金類股短線
表現將可能受到較大壓力。
即便如此，我們仍可看出世界央行對中長期增持黃金儲備抱持正向態度 ，例如在 1 月份的世界央
行黃金買家中，就意外的出現了馬來西亞央行，為該國自 2018 年以來首度增持黃金儲備，另外在 3
月初，韓國央行也宣布計畫從 2026 年第一季起，將黃金 ETF 納入其外匯準備投資組合，顯示各國
央行對於增持黃金儲備的長期方向仍未改變 。世界黃金協會也預估 ，預期全球央行在 2026 年將會增
持約 850 噸黃金，增持水準將會與去年相近，顯示未來世界央行購金買盤仍可望為金價帶來支撐。
此外，展望後市 ，金價反轉的條件實際上也已在逐步成形，當前市場直覺認為高油價將導致通膨 ，
預期聯準會動作會偏鷹，導致金價表現受到壓抑。然而若戰事在短期內結束，預料市場將忽略短期
油價上漲的影響，使降息預期大致回升到戰前水準，有利金價表現；而若美伊戰爭 進一步擴大，將
可能使油價被推升至壓抑經濟表現的價格，屆時經濟更可能面臨的問題反而不是通膨而是衰退， 聯

準會更可能降息以救經濟，隨利率走低亦有利金價反彈。故 目前來看，隨金價回檔已深，重回多頭
條件也已在逐步成形，建議短線可持續觀望戰事變化，中性看待黃金類股後市表現。

固定收益：通膨疑慮雖推升利率，未來仍有降息可能
FOMC 3/18 會議釋出訊號略偏鷹派。投票結果 11 比 1，僅 Miran 主張降息一碼，而原先偏鴿
的 Waller 轉為支持按兵不動，內部對降息的共識正在弱化。會後聲明雖新增中東局勢不確定性的描
述，釋出政策需保持耐心的訊號，但整體對經濟評估仍維持「穩健」 ，未因2 月就業數據轉弱而顯著
調整措辭。點陣圖中位數雖未變動，但分布結構出現轉變。首先，預期降息的官員人數減少，支持多
次降息的比例下滑；其次，已出現個別官員預期未來升息的情境；此外，長期利率中位數上修，顯示

中性利率評估提高。
雖然因中東能源問題導致 Fed Funds 隔夜利率交換市場所估算今年底前的利率變動預期已從戰
事爆發前的降息 2 碼大幅縮減，近期更出現不排除升息的臆測。然而，自美以伊衝突以來至 3/26(台
北時間)， 包括高盛在內的投資銀行所發布對Fed 利率(區間上緣)預期均認為 FOMC 未來兩年仍將傾
向降息。若此預期得以實現，代表市場目前已過度反映 Fed 的「鷹派情境」，之後殖利率可望有回
穩的空間。
再者，3 月油價上揚，投資人對於未來美國貨幣政策轉向的疑慮升溫，確實也導致了美國甚至是
全球的主要債券市場殖利率上揚。然而，就在市場聚焦於通膨的同時，包括 Pimco、摩根大通和
Columbia Threadneedle Investments 等投資機構 3/30 不約而同表達，投資人低估了美國對伊朗
戰爭導致經濟急劇放緩的風險的看法，並認為這場衝擊最終將引發債券殖利率再度回落 。高盛 3 月下
旬的報告提醒，比起升息，中東戰事更大的風險在於(接近)經濟衰退，這可能觸發 Fed 更大幅度的降

息。Pimco 首席投資官 Daniel Ivascyn 也表示「通常始於通膨衝擊的局面，會迅速演變為成長衝擊…
而我們正處於經濟大幅走軟的邊緣。」因此，未來債券殖利率不排除有向下的可能性存在。
隨著美國 11 月期中選舉逼近，川普在政治上將面臨更大的壓力，以美國財政體質及川普向來的
政策來看，之後美國政府勢必將盡力壓低美國公債的殖利率，有利使美債利率處於相對可控的範圍。
近期美國公債殖利率雖因市場的通膨疑慮而上揚 ， 但目前殖利率遠高於Fed 長期通膨目標(2%)，
美債的高息收對長線投資人具強大誘因 ， 因此投資人仍可以資產配置角度對於債券作適當之配置 。 此
外，包括 Pimco、摩根大通等投資機構也認為未來債券殖利率有向下的可能性存在。以目前的殖利
率看，美債仍有投資空間。考量短期殖利率受市場對 Fed 的利率政策預期變動影響較大，故在年期
與券種的選擇上可以 7~15 年的中長期美國公債或投資級債為主。

Partnership expanded across AI infrastructure, local AI computing and n
automotive: Alongside the pricing, Nvidia and MediaTek announced a deepening of
their longstanding collaboration spanning three areas, including 1) AI
infrastructure: MediaTek will adopt Nvidia’s NVLink Fusion platform to enable
customers to develop custom AI infrastructure designed to integrate with Nvidia

rack-scale systems and AI factories. 2) Local AI computing: the companies will
continue to collaborate on multiple generations of RTX Spark™ and DGX Spark™ PC
chips integrating Nvidia GPUs with MediaTek SoCs. 3) Automotive: MediaTek and
Nvidia will continue developing platforms for AI-powered, software-defined vehicles
in the era of physical AI.
Key highlights from the joint interview
Following the announcement, the CEOs of Nvidia and MediaTek were interviewed jointly

by Bloomberg TV. Below we summarize key highlights from the interview.
Nvidia characterized the partnership with MediaTek as a multi-generation and n
long-term collaboration. Nvidia commented that the two companies are working
together across multiple generations of edge AI and PC SoCs, while the expanded
partnership will also cover data-center XPUs, networking and robotic systems.
Nvidia added that it is a decade-long roadmap.
The expanded collaboration will allow MediaTek’s XPUs to connect directly into n

Nvidia’s broader AI factory architecture. Nvidia noted that it will connect its
NVLink Fusion scale-up system, Spectrum-X switches and broader networking
infrastructure to MediaTek’s XPUs.  According to Nvidia, this would allow MediaTek
XPUs to be integrated into data centers already running Nvidia infrastructure, while
MediaTek XPU project wins could also create opportunities for Nvidia networking
products.
MediaTek expects the partnership to support growth in its AI ASIC business, n

although the new revenue contribution will take time. When asked whether the
collaboration could accelerate MediaTek’s AI chip business, MediaTek said that it
expects this to happen, but noted that it would take some time as the company is
only beginning to integrate Nvidia’s latest NVLink version into the relevant chassis.
MediaTek highlighted faster time-to-market for customers as a key benefit.
Both companies indicated that customer engagement around NVLink Fusion has n

already started. Nvidia said the collaboration was in large part driven by customer
requests and that there are already customers committed to NVLink Fusion.
Nvidia positioned NVLink Fusion as a way to accommodate custom XPUs within n
its existing infrastructure rather than as a replacement for its broader platform.
Nvidia noted that XPUs are already present in the market and said it is opening its
platform to allow customers’ specialized XPUs to connect more easily to Nvidia

infrastructure. Nvidia added that it expects to continue participating through
networking, switching and CPUs, even in AI factories where some racks incorporate
XPUs.

### Page 3

Nvidia indicated that the partnership enables MediaTek to offer a more n
complete end-to-end XPU solution. Nvidia noted that an XPU provider would
otherwise need to address multiple system-level components, including NVLink
switching, NICs, Ethernet switching and scale-up/scale-out networking. Through the
partnership, these components can be provided through Nvidia’s existing
infrastructure.
MediaTek plans to use the bond proceeds across supply-chain and technology n

investments. MediaTek said supply-chain investment will be one use of proceeds
and emphasized that the company’s supply chain is global. The company also plans
to invest further in design capabilities, ASIC chip capabilities, IP and interconnects,
and advanced packaging, with the proceeds supporting the expanded Nvidia
collaboration.
The collaboration also extends to Nvidia’s custom HBM architecture. Nvidia n
indicated that its next-generation custom HBM will use an Nvidia-designed base

layer that has been extended to NVLink Fusion partners, including MediaTek and
Amazon. Nvidia noted that the NVHBM and XPU die will be integrated into a CoWoS
package, allowing partners to leverage Nvidia’s installed base and supply-chain
scale.
MediaTek identified the supply chain as its primary bottleneck. MediaTek said n
supply-chain challenges extend beyond HBM to substrates and other components,
while noting that its location in Taiwan and close collaboration with Nvidia are

helpful. Nvidia added that its networking ecosystem is becoming part of MediaTek’s
supply chain, while MediaTek’s XPU is becoming part of Nvidia’s supply chain.
Nvidia sees a solid standalone business case in its MediaTek investment. In n
response to questions on financing, Nvidia noted that MediaTek operates its own
independent business and is already a highly profitable and successful company.
Nvidia indicated the investment is grounded in the companies’ deep, multi-year

technology partnership, with the current roadmap extending over a decade, and is
intended to demonstrate Nvidia’s confidence in and commitment to the
collaboration. Nvidia also expects the investment to generate attractive returns.
Our thoughts - Strong ecosystem endorsement, broader system-level
opportunity and potential new TAM into custom HBM base die
Strong endorsement from two leading AI ecosystem partners
We believe the most immediate takeaway is the strong endorsement from both Nvidia

and Alphabet. Nvidia subscribed to US$3.5bn of MediaTek’s US$3.9bn CB issuance,
while Alphabet and global institutional investors participated in the remaining
US$400mn. In our view, Nvidia’s capital commitment provides strong validation of
MediaTek’s silicon, connectivity and advanced packaging capabilities. Alphabet’s
participation also reinforces MediaTek’s relevance across multiple major AI ecosystems.
Funding should strengthen supply-chain security and support continued technology

investment. According to the company, one of the original purposes of the financing
was to secure capacity and supply for constrained components such as HBM.
Although certain customers currently procure HBM and other components directly, we

believe MediaTek could assume greater supply-chain responsibility under future
engagement models, including entering into capacity or supply agreements on behalf of
customer projects.
The company also indicated that it plans to invest further in its technology and design
capabilities, including ASIC design, IP , interconnects and advanced packaging, etc. We
believe the additional funding should provide MediaTek with greater flexibility to
secure the resources required for increasingly complex AI related programs.

The partnership broadens MediaTek’s role from custom AI silicon toward system-level
enablement
Based on the interview and the company announcement, we believe MediaTek is seeking
to extend its role beyond designing and delivering an individual AI ASIC toward helping
customers address a broader portion of the system surrounding the XPU. This could
include connectivity, memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and
integration with rack-scale AI infrastructure.

However, we do not believe this means MediaTek intends to build or sell complete
AI racks. Rather, MediaTek can help customers integrate their custom XPUs into
Nvidia’s pre-validated chassis, networking and rack-scale architectures, allowing
customers to deploy and scale their AI infrastructure more efficiently.
NVLink Fusion is intended to provide customers with a faster path from custom XPU to
AI factory. Customers can focus their resources on the differentiated compute portion of

the accelerator while relying on Nvidia and MediaTek for surrounding technologies such
as NVLink connectivity, high-speed SerDes, memory architecture, packaging,
manufacturing and system qualification.
Instead of independently engineering and qualifying every component surrounding the
custom XPU, customers can use NVIDIA ’s prebuilt, prequalified and system-prevalidated
platform as a starting point. We believe this should reduce integration complexity and

risk, shorten time to market and make it easier for customers to deploy custom XPUs
within existing Nvidia-based data centers and scale them into rack-level AI factories.
Incremental revenue will likely take time, but the partnership expands MediaTek’s
longer-term opportunity
MediaTek and Nvidia did not provide a timeline for the first NVLink Fusion-related
customer, product or revenue contribution. The company indicated that it is only
beginning to integrate the latest version of NVLink, after which customers would still

need to complete their own product development, qualification and deployment
processes. We therefore see limited immediate earnings implications and believe the
incremental opportunity is more likely to emerge over the medium to long term.
Nevertheless, the partnership broadens MediaTek’s addressable opportunity from
designing an individual AI ASIC toward enabling a production-ready system. We
believe this could improve customer win rates and increase MediaTek’s potential content

and value capture per project through additional contributions from connectivity,
memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and system integration.

### Page 5

The relationship extends across the full edge-to-cloud AI computing stack 
Beyond AI infrastructure, the partnership covers multiple generations of DGX Spark and 
RTX Spark chips for local AI computing, as well as automotive platforms. We therefore 
view the announcement as a broader, multi-generation technology alignment spanning 
cloud AI factories, enterprise and local AI computing, PCs and physical AI, rather than a 
single-product collaboration. 
Management noted that the Nvidia partnership should not restrict MediaTek’s existing 
custom ASIC engagements. We see NVLink Fusion as an additional architecture option 
rather than an exclusive platform. Customers will be able to decide whether to adopt the 
Nvidia ecosystem depending on their infrastructure and workload requirements, while 
MediaTek can continue supporting customers using other architectures. 
In our view, Alphabet’s participation in the CB issuance should allay any concerns 
some investors may have had that the closer Nvidia relationship could affect 
MediaTek’s existing hyperscaler engagements. Instead, we believe MediaTek’s 
positioning is to remain architecture-flexible while providing customers with a broader 
range of design and system-integration options. 
NVHBM collaboration extends MediaTek’s participation into the memory and 
packaging architecture 
We note that Nvidia’s next-generation NVHBM will use a custom HBM base layer 
designed by Nvidia, which has also been extended to NVLink Fusion partners including 
MediaTek and Amazon. Nvidia noted that NVHBM will be integrated with the XPU die in 
a CoWoS package, allowing partners to leverage NVIDIA ’s broader HBM and packaging 
supply chain. While the NVHBM base layer itself is NVIDIA-designed, we believe 
MediaTek’s participation could deepen its involvement in memory architecture, 
interconnect and advanced packaging integration for XPU programs. Separately, 
MediaTek has highlighted custom HBM and memory-interface capabilities as part 
of its broader XPU offering, which we believe could provide incremental content 
opportunities for customer-specific designs outside Nvidia’s standardized NVHBM 
implementation. 
Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

•Broad-base 2Q, cost-capped margin. Mgmt. reiterated that 2Q26 growth 
was broad-based across every product line, end market and region, not a 
single AI pocket. GPM was slightly below Street, as raw material cost 
inflation offset part of the pricing and mix improvement. Internal 
manufacturing efficiency projects are underway to absorb it in the future.
•Jul price hike hits spot and direct. The Jul price adjustment covers spot 
prices and direct customers, vs. earlier rounds limited to contract 
customers, and spans c.50% of revenue including MLCC, tantalum and 
other capacitors. Spot pricing flows through immediately rather than at 
renewal, so mgmt. expects ASP and revenue effects shortly, with margin 
impact far more visible in 2H. Mgmt. also sees no sign of demand slowing 
down in the near-term.
•Tantalum now the largest line. AI is not an MLCC-only story for passive 
components. Tantalum grew fastest of any line in 2Q, suits AI power 
management, and has over taken magnetics to become Yageo's largest 
product line. MLCC also grew well and should keep growing in coming 
quarters. AI revenue is now 16% of total revenue, spread across tantalums, 
MLCCs, resistors and inductors rather than single product.
•65 sites, dual-region by product. Every product is manufactured in at least 
two regions, giving customers flexibility against tariff and geopolitical risks. 
Mgmt. believe the existing 65 facilities are flexible in adding new lines at a 
fast pace whenever shortages appear, saving customers 1-2 years vs. 
building from greenfield. The company also planned the capacity expansion 
ahead of demand.

### Page 2

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•YFIII and overseas MLCC bases. YFIII in Kaohsiung, commissioned in 2024, is Yageo's largest MLCC fab and still has
space for additional MLCC lines, with AI-specific equipment arriving monthly since last year. Outside Taiwan, Yageo
has been opening new lines within existing facilities in Mexico and China, leaving it able to absorb incremental AI
orders without a new building cycle.
•Lead times extend, LTA interest rises. Lead time of most of the products has lengthened vs. 1H26. Tantalum is the

tightest, followed by MLCC and resistors. Mgmt. indicated all AI-related lines are extremely tight, and more
customers are now approaching Yageo to sign LTAs.
•NP0 leadership on the power side. Yageo estimates a meaningful share (at least 30-40%) of NP0 high-voltage
MLCC, based on the internal assessment. Built on EV and industrial experience, NP0 fits AI's high-voltage, high-
temp duty and sits on the DC power side, while high-cap MLCC serves the near-chip stage. Yageo ships direct to

leading power supply players and is invited as first batch of design partners.
•Low-to-high conversion only partial. Converting low-end MLCC capacity to high-end is only partially possible.
High-spec MLCC stores more capacitance through higher layer counts, requiring advanced stackers rather than
conventional equipment. Yageo can optimize and redeploy some existing tooling, but not fully substitute, which is
why it is committing capex to new AI production line instead of relying on conversion.

Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 3

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

銷售 ， 產品主要應用於手持式電動工具 、 園
林工具 、 工業用縫紉機及自動化設備等 ， 主
要銷售市場為美國、歐洲及中國大陸等地
區。 產品應用領域以電動工具佔比最高
75%， 園林工具領域佔比11.5%， 公司齒輪
精密度以 9 級以上的中高精密度齒輪為主。

主要客戶：TTI、松下、牧田、喜利得 
主要競爭對手：

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

鈞興-KY (4571 TT) 
成長潛力醞釀中

永豐觀點 
電動工具與園林工具溫和成長 ，人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手有機會成為未來新成
長動能。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：理由包括：(1) 電動工具與園林工具市場每年成長 4%-6%，今年下半
年電動工具有新品鋪貨，全年營收個位數成長，預估2027 年亦維持溫和成長，(2) 
智能傳動今年營收將成長 21%，其中工業機器人齒輪接單熱絡，預料 2027 年成
長動能持續 ，(3)人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手零件送樣中 ， 若通過將於2027 年量
產，將是未來成長的另一波推手。在評價方面，品牌廠 TTI、Stanley Blac 本益比
已來到 16-20 倍，公司營運穩健成長，人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手有機會成為
新成長動能，維持買進建議，目標價 220 元（20 X 2027EPS）。

公司簡介：鈞興為高精度中小模數齒輪製造商，產品為各式齒輪、齒輪箱及五金件
之設計、製造及銷售，產品主要應用於手持式電動工具、園林工具、工業用縫紉機
及自動化設備等，主要銷售市場為美國、歐洲及中國大陸等地區。客戶幾乎皆為該
行業的國際領導品牌，客戶包括 TTI、松下、牧田、喜利得等等。公司從 OEM 開
始製作銷售平車齒輪，以台資廠商技術，給予比日本廠商更有競爭力的價格，再從
縫紉機跨足其他領域深耕技術優勢，延伸到電動工具、園林工具，營運模式也從
OEM 轉為 ODM。產品應用領域以電動工具佔比最高 75%，園林工具領域佔比
11.5%，公司齒輪精密度以 9 級以上的中高精密度齒輪為主。 
鈞興 2Q26 EPS 為 1.72 元，業外因人民幣升值而有匯兌損失：鈞興-KY 2Q26 合
併營收為 7.28 億元（+8.15%YoY） ，毛利率34.24%，較去年同期提升 3.92 個百
分點，主要原因是(1) 產品結構優化，毛利率相對較低的齒輪箱與粉末冶金佔比下
降，其他產品如智能傳動等比重上升，(2) 越南工廠生產銷售的規模經濟發酵，毛
利率提升，上述兩因素抵銷人民幣升值的不利因素。營業費用較高，乃因庫存股轉
讓給員工費用達 3185 萬元，高於去年同期的 1280 萬元。營業利 1.11 億元，業
外有人民幣升值帶來的匯兌損失 1581 萬元，稅後淨利 0.91 億元（-0.68%YoY），
EPS 為 1.72 元。 
下半年電動工具新品出貨，營收將個位數成長；電動工具約有 30%-40%應用在房
地產相關領域，受美國通膨、升息預期影響，客戶下單並未太積極 （不過終端庫存
也不多） ，然客戶下半年將推出新品，初期鋪貨量會較多，綜合上述兩因素，下半
年電動工具預計個位數成長，園林工具則持平至小幅衰退。展望 2027 年，預期電
動工具維持個位數成長，園林工具在正常庫存回補下，有機會慢慢好轉。 
智能傳動今年營收將成長 21%，其中佔比最大的工業機器人齒輪成長勢頭將延續
至 2027 年：智能傳動 1H26 佔比 10.44%，產品包括工業機器人齒輪、減速工具
齒輪、諧波減速器與自行車中置電機 ，預計智能傳動全年營收將成長 21%YoY： 
(1) 工業機器人齒輪 1H26 營收 1.26 億元，佔營收比重 8.33%，成長 46%YoY，
兩家大客戶 （日本與中國） 是成長動能所在。2025 年該產品營收 1.78 億元，目前
接單已有 2.6 億元，不過部分是明年上半年出貨，今年預計出貨額可達 2.3 億元。
由於自動化行業需求暢旺，2027 年工業機器人齒輪成長動能將持續。另外，公司
將與該日本客戶合作，打入其他日本客戶齒輪供應鏈中。(2) 減速工具齒輪客戶為
德系自動化設備廠商，2022 年俄烏戰爭後自動化設備開始衰退，去年下半年減幅
縮小，今年出貨好轉，預計可成長 10%。(3) 諧波減速器 1H26 營收 323 萬元，
目前已接單 2000 萬元，預計今年營收可達 1300 萬元（去年 900-1000 萬元） 。
(4)自行車中置電機訂單狀況未達預期，目前訂單有將外銷日本的小徑車，但仍在
等待取得日本電子認證，預計 2027 年將有數百台的出貨量。另外公司將開發電子
無段變速系統，產品將於明年正式亮相。 
人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手零件送樣中，若通過將於2027 年量產：人形機器人
目前接洽的進度包括(1)應用在醫療的台灣廠商，產品為關節模組零件， 客戶進行
測試中，(2)深圳一家系統整合商關節模組採用公司漸開線少齒差產品，可克服使
用諧波減速機所產生的散熱問題，目前送樣給客戶測試，需耗時 2-3 個月，若通
過，依客戶規格再修正後有機會於 2027 年量產，(3) 微型齒輪應用在靈巧手將送
樣給美系客戶，若通過可於 2027 年量產，2028 年需購地擴充產能。 
產能狀況 —越南二期 4Q26 將陸續進設備： 在產能方面，珠海與越南廠產能約
70%：30%，珠海工廠電動與園林工具產能利用率八成，機器人相關產能利用率九
成至九成五；越南廠下半年訂單狀況依舊好，但今年招工比較困難，產能利用率超

營運現況與分析

過 100%；越南二期九月底完工、第四季準備進設備，估計四至五年才會滿載。

聯茂(6213) 
買進, TP 670 (潛在報酬 31.37%)  
買進 
Price(09/08):510 
Target Price：670

展望後市， 聯茂受惠於一般型伺服器與 AI 伺服器需求持續升溫，
帶動 CCL 出貨動能強勁。為反映上游原物料（如 E-Glass 玻纖
布、高階銅箔） 嚴重供不應求與價格輪番上揚，公司啟動之漸進式
報價調漲將於下半年持續擴大發酵 ， 龐大的低基期槓桿效益將有效
推升產品平均售價 ， 並大幅修復中低階產品之毛利率 。隨一般伺服
器迎來 CPU 平台世代交替，將直接驅動材料由  M6 升級至 M7
（單價較 M6 顯著高出 30% 至 50%） ；加上低軌衛星（LEO）
地面接收站進入放量期，以及美系  AI GPU 廠高階專案預計於  
2027 年陸續放量，產品組合結構性優化將大幅推升營運規模與獲
利天花板。綜合以上，研究部預估聯茂  2026 年 EPS 為 18.74 
元；2027 年 EPS 為 44.80 元。考量公司長線受惠伺服器平台
規格升級、 新興應用放量與報價調漲紅利 ， 獲利成長趨勢明確且具
備爆發力； 加上目前評價面相較同業仍處於低位階 ， 研究部給予目
標價 670 元（15x 2027 EPS） ，投資建議為買進。  
⚫ 近期投資評等 公司基本資料 
聯茂電子（6213）成立於 1997 年，為全球主要的銅箔基板（CCL）與
黏合片 （Prepreg） 製造商之一。公司具備深厚的樹脂配方開發與壓合技
術，產品線涵蓋廣泛，能同時滿足成熟市場與高階應用的多元需求。在
營收方面，聯茂持續穩定供應消費性電子、車用及一般工業所需之常規
型基板（如傳統 FR4 及中低階耗損材料） ，維持龐大的基礎市佔率。另
一方面，為因應雲端運算與資料中心的高速傳輸需求，公 司近年積極將
產品重心向高頻高速 （Low Dk/Df） 材料推進 。 其自主研發的高階材料 （包
含 M6 至 M9 等級）已逐步擴大導入 AI 伺服器、高階運算（HPC）、
新一代交換器（3.2T）與低軌衛星（LEO）等前瞻應用領域，以滿足終
端硬體對於極低訊號損耗與高散熱的嚴苛標準。

在產能佈局上，聯茂透過台灣、中國及泰國等多地廠區進行彈性調度。
隨著終端客戶對 AI 伺服器與高階材料的規格升級（如 PCIe Gen6 ），
公司亦持續擴充泰國廠等海外高階產能，並積極推進石英布（ Q布） 、
PTFE 等次世代基板技術的研發與認證，以因應未來電子產業規格升級
的長期趨勢。

股票股利 0 漲價效益發酵與產品組合優化，第二季毛利率倍增、獲利大幅跳升 
聯茂近期公佈  2026 年第二季財報，合併營收為  115.30 億元，季增 
26.11%、年增 29.88%。營收擴張之主因，來自上游  E-Glass 玻纖布
與銅箔供應吃緊，公司於第二季落實報價調漲，單季累積漲幅達 30%。
此外，一般型伺服器需求維持穩健，佔營收比重約 55.5%，亦為整體出
貨規模提供基本盤支撐。

獲利結構方面，第二季毛利率由首季的 11.42% 回升至 22.02%，營業
利益率亦提 升至  16.03% ，單季營業利益達  18.48 億 元 ， 季 增 
335.67%、年增 134.51%。毛利率與營利率顯著擴張，主要受惠於單季 
30% 的報價漲幅大幅改善佔營收大宗（M4 以下佔約 70%）之中低階
產品利差，搭配營收規模放大稀釋固定折舊費用，進而推升本業獲利。

業外及稅率方面，第二季淨營業外支出為 1,427 萬元，主要為認列出售
中國小型壓合 PCB 廠之處分損失；單季有效稅率則因中國廠盈餘匯回
以支應無錫新廠擴建，上升至 30.2%。綜合以上，第二季歸屬於母公司
業主稅後淨利達  12.79 億元，季增 306.76%、年增 204.29%，單季 
EPS 達 3.52 元。

七、八月營收連創新高，漲價效益與伺服器需求推升第三季營運 
聯茂 8 月營收達 50.44 億元，月增 6.21%、年增 97.93%，連續兩個
月改寫單月營收新高 ； 累計前八月營收達 304.66 億元 ， 年增 42.25%。
展望第三季，受惠於上游玻纖布（E-Glass）與銅箔供應持續緊缺，公司
順利向客戶轉嫁成本，帶動第三季報價持續調漲 （預估單季漲幅達 15% 
至 20% ） ；此外，一般型伺服器終端需求維持強勁，且公司於美系主力 
CPU 平台供應鏈中維持高市佔率 ， 出貨動能無虞 ， 推升整體營收維持強
勁季增軌道。

獲利方面，第三季雖將認列員工分紅與認股獎金，預期將墊高單季營業
費用，然受惠於報價調漲之槓桿效益持續發酵，然受惠於報價持續 調漲
帶動營收規模顯著放大，將有效稀釋營業費用率，抵銷費用端之負擔。
此外，缺料狀況微幅改善帶動稼動率穩步回升，且漲價效益持續修復佔
營收大宗之中低階產品（M4 以下）利差，預期第三季毛利率將自前季
之 22.02% 顯著擴張至 27.50%，本業獲利結構保持強勁。

綜合以上 ， 研究部預估聯茂第三季營收為 150.76 億元 ， 季增 30.75%、
年增 95.17%；毛利率預估為 27.50%，較前季持續呈現顯著擴張；歸
除息日 2026/07/02

3 
屬母公司稅後淨利為  21.75 億元，季增  70.04%、年增 570%，單季 
EPS 預估為 5.98 元。

報價調漲效益與高階材料滲透率拉升，推升 2026 與 2027 營運規模 
展望後市，在伺服器基礎建設方面，受惠於一般型伺服器 CPU 平台世
代交替，雖受限於 IC 測試進度而遞延至今年底或明年初放量，然終端
需求維持強勁，且聯茂於美系主力 CPU 平台 （AMD）之 ODM 供應鏈
中掌握高達 70% 之市佔率，出貨動能無虞。此平台轉換將直接驅動材
料由 M6 升級至 M7， 考量 M7 產品單價 （ASP）較 M6 顯著高出 30% 
至 50%，此規格轉換將大幅挹注營收規模，帶動 M6 以上高階產品佔
比於 2027 年進一步攀升至 35% 至 40%。 此外 ， 針對次世代 AI 伺服
器 ， 公司已全面將資源集中於 M9 等級材料 ， 其產品已於 2025 年底順
利通過美系 GPU 大廠認證；考量 M9 毛利率高達 50% 以上，預期於 
2027 年第二季起隨終端客戶放量 ， 僅需達成低個位數之營收滲透率 ， 即
可對整體毛利帶來顯著拉抬效果。

除伺服器應用外，低軌衛星（LEO）亦為 2027 年另一成長動能。聯茂
已於今年下半年順利通過美系指標客戶 （SpaceX）認證，預期相關專案
將於 2027 年上半年陸續發酵。初期將以衛星地面接收站之材料需求
（M4、M6 等級為主）先行放量，後續將進一步延伸至天上衛星所需之
極低損耗高階材料（M7、M8） ，為明年增添穩健之獲利支柱。

針對原物料供需與報價走勢，因 E-Glass 玻纖布極度短缺，且高階銅箔
產能排擠效應顯現，預期供不應求態勢將延續至 2027 年中旬。為反映
成本，公司下半年持續落實報價調漲，第三、第四季每季皆維持約 15% 
- 20% 之漲幅，並將於 2027 年上半年維持微幅上調趨勢。在稼動率部
分，隨中國玻纖布供應商產能於年底微幅釋出，預估第四季產能利用率
可望由 70% 至 75% 回升至 78% 左右 ， 並於明年下半年逐步朝 85% 
邁進。。

產能擴充與長線資本支出方面，考量前段關鍵設備（上膠機）交期長達 
1.5 至 2 年，公司採取集中於下半年開出之穩健擴產節奏。2026 年底
總產能將達 570 萬張/月；2027 年底將於無錫廠擴增 60 萬張 ， 達 630 
萬張/月；2028 年配合美系 ASIC 客戶長線專案放量，無錫廠與泰國廠
將分別再開出 135 萬張與 60 萬張 ， 年底總產能將達 825 萬張/月 ， 為
長線高階板材需求奠定產能基礎。

4 
結合以上，研究部預估聯茂  2026 年全年營收為  534.02 億元，年增 
61.34%，毛利率達 24.39%，歸屬母公司稅後淨利為  68.12 億元，年
增 351.03%， 全年 EPS 為 18.74 元；2027 年隨一般型伺服器 M7 滲
透率翻倍、AI 伺服器 M9 材料實質放量，加上低軌衛星專案啟動，全
年 營收 預估達  838.22 億元， 年增 56.96%，毛 利率 進一步 攀升至  
31.77%，歸屬母公司稅後淨利達  162.87 億元，年增 139.11%，全年 
EPS 預估為 44.80 元。

結論與建議 : 
展望後市，聯茂受惠於一般型伺服器與 AI 伺服器需求持續升溫，帶動 
CCL 出貨動能強勁。為反映上游原物料（如 E-Glass 玻纖布、高階銅
箔）嚴重供不應求與價格輪番上揚，公司啟動之漸進式報價調漲將於下
半年持續擴大發酵，龐大的低基期槓桿效益將有效推升產品平均售價，
並大幅修復中低階產品之毛利率。隨一般伺服器迎來 CPU 平台世代交
替 ， 將直接驅動材料由 M6 升級至 M7（單價較 M6 顯著高出 30% 至 
50%） ； 加上低軌衛星 （LEO） 地面接收站進入放量期 ， 以及美系 AI GPU 
廠高階專案預計於 2027 年陸續放量，產品組合結構性優化將大幅推升
營運規模與獲利天花板。綜合以上，研究部預估聯茂 2026 年 EPS 為 
18.74 元；2027 年 EPS 為 44.80 元。考量公司長線受惠伺服器平台
規格升級、新興應用放量與報價調漲紅利，獲利成長趨勢明確且具備爆
發力；加上目前評價面相較同業仍處於低位階，研究部給予目標價 670 
元（15x 2027 EPS） ，投資建議為買進。

兆聯實業 (6944 TT)

高科技產業擴廠帶動公司營運續揚

投資評等維持買進，目標價 976 元，潛在漲幅 37% 
1. 毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
2. 在手訂單續創新高 
3. 因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長

毛利率跳升帶動 2Q26 獲利優於預期 
兆聯實業 2Q26 營收為 50.8 億元，YoY+36.1%，略低於預期。其中工程類營收為
39.3 億元(YoY+35.0%)，占比 77%，主要係因在手工程持續去化；服務類營收為
11.5 億元(YoY+40.3%)， 成長轉趨強勁主要係因2H25 開始有一個新的製程使用新
的耗材所致，占比 23%。受惠於 1)美國專案工程執行效率佳，完工進度較預期時間
短、2)新承做專案因業主急於擴廠，對施工進度的考量甚於成本，故公司的接單毛
利率較高，與 3)去年接單時台幣較為強勢，今年台幣趨貶，故 2Q26 工程毛利率上
揚至 28%( vs. 1Q26 的 26.5%)，服務類毛利率則達 30.1% (vs. 1Q26 的 29.9%)，
帶動整體毛利率達 28.4%，優於預期。因此，稅後淨利達 8.7 億元，YoY+70.8%，
亦高於研究部預期。若以除權後股本計算，EPS 為 8.75 元。

在手訂單續創新高 
受惠於 AI 需求強勁，半導體、記憶體、PCB、ABF 產業建廠積極度高，兆聯實業 6
月底在手訂單餘額達 277 億元，分別為國內 73%、美國 25%、新加坡 7%、中國
<5%。公司並於 7 月發布重訊公告接獲美國 50 億元以上訂單，若含此訂單，美國
占比將提升至 40%。 由於在手訂單續創新高 ， 研究部正向看待未來工程類營收成長 ，
並預估 3Q26 營收為 59.6 億元，YoY+24.5%。基於 3Q26 工程類毛利率可望持平
於 2Q26 之假設，預估稅後淨利為 10.4 億元，YoY+51.9%，EPS 為 10.4 元。

因應國內外需求，持續擴增產能將推升營運成長 
兆聯實業近期公告將發行兩檔無擔保轉換公司債，合計金額為新台幣 25 億元，主
要用於支應美國營運資金需求 ， 預估此對股權稀釋比例約3%。 因應客戶建廠需求 ，
為取得資本財與服務財優勢，公司於台灣中南部有購置土地或建廠計畫，資本支出
約 10 億元；海外方面，公司將投入 19.5 億元建置美國新廠(第一期)，此廠可望在
2028 年完工。雖然業主隨著發包案量提升而有培養其他供應商的計畫，然因兆聯
實業已與業主合作多年，建廠配合默契佳，與國際競爭者相較亦 有成本費用優勢，
故公司競爭性仍佳。除了優化工程進度以提升毛利率外，公司目前已切入記憶體、
先進封裝、載板的純水系統，因純水系統的毛利率較高，未來若能切入半導體製造
廠的純水系統，則毛利率將再提升，另外，美國新廠完成後，公司服務類營收將加
速成長，毛利率亦將隨之提升。

富邦不同之處 
富邦 2026/2027 EPS(F) 為 38.54/48.79 元，Bloomberg 預估為 37.74/41.11 元。

評價與風險 
兆聯實業聚焦在高科技廠水資源工程，因半導體大客戶先進製程擴展不停歇，將帶
動業績再創新高。評價風險為半導體產業擴張放緩，以及原料與人工成本飆漲。

持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

Document 199: Capitalism and the dollar_ the AI race, tokenization, and the dollar's future (2026-09-04)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capitalism and the dollar_ the AI race, tokenization, and the dollar's future (2026-09-04).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 15
Status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Capitalism and the dollar: the AI race, 
tokenization, and the dollar's future

1 
Authors 
Mallika Sachdeva 
Strategist

September 03, 2026 
A massive race for capital 
The US is sprinting in its reach for capital. Private markets, debt, and public 
equities have been setting records, and the US is enjoying unprecedented 
amounts of foreign equity inflows. In this paper, we discuss how the US is 
leveraging the power of capitalism – supercharged by blockchain – to try and win 
the AI race. But the US reach for capital is not only about AI spending; it also 
reflects growing fiscal deficits and the need for foreign private funding. The US is 
increasing its reliance on private capital to offset public sector vulnerabilities. 
Meanwhile, China is running a very different race – given large trade surpluses, 
domestic excess savings, and a "low-cost" AI approach, China is raising less 
capital, with much less from abroad. 
America embraces asset tokenization 
The US is using blockchain technology to support US dollar and capital market 
dominance. We wrote last year about the crucial role of stablecoins. We are now 
at the next stage: asset tokenization – converting ownership rights to financial 
assets into digital tokens recorded on blockchain. Asset tokenization is not 
futuristic – it is happening now at the heart of US markets. Securities 
depositories and exchanges are building the infrastructure to tokenize US assets, 
with regulatory oversight. 
TINA meets Tokenization: the future of the dollar 
TINA already plays a key role attracting capital to the US. Tokenization could add 
to this by making US assets more attractive and accessible. The collateral 
velocity of tokenized assets could be a superpower. And if US assets are trading 
24/7 with instant settlement, this could impact global behaviour, particularly for 
retail. But lowering the hurdles for inbound capital also exposes the US to capital 
going out. The dollar is becoming riskier, more equity sensitive, and leveraged to 
the AI race. If AI proves uneconomical, or the US starts to fall behind, the dollar is 
very exposed. 
Open markets meet Open technology: the future of capitalism 
The US is taking an “open markets-closed tech” approach to the AI race, while 
China is taking an “open tech-closed markets” one. The US is widening the doors 
for capital but keeping technology closed to preserve corporate pricing power. 
Meanwhile, China is pushing out low-cost models with open-weights. This is not 
just a contest between AI models, but economic models. If China's approach 
undercuts US profits, this could threaten the US model of shareholder capitalism 
itself. China's challenge is not only about technology, but the central maxim of 
US power: that free and open capital markets always deliver the winning 
innovation.

### Page 2

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Page 2 Deutsche Bank AG
Part 1: A massive reach for capital in the US
The US is sprinting at record speed in its reach for capital. Huge sums of money are
being raised to fund the buildout of AI infrastructure and development of frontier AI
models, with US companies expected to spend around $800bn in AI capex this year.
We argue in this paper that the US is leveraging the full power of capitalism to fund
capital investments seen as crucial to winning the AI race

The US is raising enormous amounts of private capital
We simplify the key trends in the US' capital-raising landscape into a few
observations. First, AI labs are turning to private markets to raise large sums of
equity capital. Second, hyperscalers are turning to investment grade debt
markets to issue meaningful amounts of debt for the first time. And third, public
equity markets are back in focus for capital funding with some of the world's
biggest companies returning to issue stock, and major IPOs in the pipeline.

n The AI venture capital industry has raised over $400bn this year alone, with
over 90% of the biggest deal volumes in the US (Pitchbook). The two largest
AI labs in the US have raised a combined $217bn this year – valuing each
company near $1tn. We note that xAI had raised $20bn earlier in the year,
before being acquired by SpaceX in February. On an annualized basis, AI VC
capital is running at three times last year's pace.
n In debt capital markets, the shift has also been staggering. While free cash

flows remain an important source of funding for hyperscalers, the capital
intensity of the infrastructure buildout is outrunning cash flows, forcing
companies to turn to debt. Hyperscalers like Google, Meta, Oracle and
Amazon raised an average of just USD25bn per year between them over
2020-24. In 2026 YTD, they have raised around ten times that amount
through IG credit markets. Microsoft has yet to turn to debt markets.
n In public equity markets, Google also had its first primary equity raise since

its 2004 IPO in June 2026, raising $85bn. Returning to issue is a big reversal
from years of buying back stock. This is also setting up to be a historic year
for IPOs. SpaceX achieved the largest total market valuation of a newly
listed company in history near $2tn in its June 2026 IPO. The largest US AI
labs are expected to come to the IPO market soon which could change the
AI boom as we have written about here.

China is running a different race
On many metrics, Chinese models are closing the gap to the US in terms of
performance and capabilities (Figure 3). While the US remains ahead in frontier
development, Chinese models account for more than half of US company token
usage on Open Router (Yahoo Finance).
But from a capital perspective, China appears to be running a different race -
raising much less capital, less from private sources, and less from abroad. While

US companies are expected to spend around $800bn in AI capex this year, that
number is closer to $100bn in China. While the biggest US AI labs have raised nearly
$250bn in private funding rounds this year, Chinese labs have raised far less. The
largest capital raise came from DeepSeek which raised $7.4bn earlier in the year,
and may be returning for a further $8bn. China's National AI fund has participated
in a number of capital raisings. Stanford University's recent AI report made a similar

observation: noting that in 2025, US private AI investment was 23 times the
$12.4bn invested in China, although Chinese numbers are likely understated given
government guided funds.
The stark difference in the approach to capital raising is also reflected in markets.
With US AI labs raising far more capital for ownership stakes, the market valuations
of US AI companies compared with their Chinese peers are many orders of
magnitude apart (Figure 4). Furthermore, while Chinese hyperscalers like Tencent,

Baidu and Alibaba have been tapping debt markets, they are doing so in smaller
size, with a no major uptick compared with earlier years. They are, however,
increasingly funding in dim sum bonds, suggestive of a desire to de-dollarize
funding. See more on the Rise of the RMB Financing Ecosystem here.

The US will likely want all the foreign capital it can get
The dramatic reach for capital in the US is not only reflective of an American

economic model that prioritizes open capital markets, it also reflects the US'
growing need for foreign financing given the US' large twin deficit position. The
US is running a 6%+ fiscal deficit combined with a near 4% of GDP current account
deficit. In the absence of fiscal savings, incremental private sector capex will
require funding from abroad. At a time when private sector financing requirements
for AI capex are rising, it is not a surprise the US is widening its reach for capital from

abroad.

### Page 4

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Page 4 Deutsche Bank AG
The US is in fact enjoying an unprecedented amount of equity capital inflow. In Q2
2026 alone, the US received over USD400bn of foreign equity capital, well above
any previous quarter. The amount of capital coming into US equities is also well
above what US equity performance alone would imply (Figure 5), and now well
above debt flows ( Figure 6) – the historical mainstay of US capital account

financing.
We have written in the past about the big rotation underway in US financing.
Geopolitical ruptures in long-standing alliances in defence and trade are hurting
long-term appetite for USD government debt, particularly by the foreign official
sector. But at the same time, technology is pulling enormous equity capital in from
other types of foreign investors like retail. The gap between net equity flows to the
US (increasing) and debt flows (falling) has never been wider, and the shift from

official to private funding sources is becoming more noticeable. With the US on the
cusp of a potentially significant IPO cycle, we find it notable that US companies are
picking up on the funding shift. SpaceX reportedly initially earmarked 30% of its IPO
for retail investors, more than three times as much as a traditional IPO offering.
As we show in this paper, the US will likely remain very focused on attracting
foreign private capital to fund both its AI ambitions and its deficits.

Where blockchain technology comes in
The US is taking a very capital-intensive approach to the AI race, raising record
amounts of capital, and attracting huge amounts of it from overseas. Foreign
capital not only helps support the dollar by providing deficit funding, it also

improves the cost of financing for US companies. It is natural for the US to leverage
technology that supports these capital flow trends.
We have written at length about how the US is using the power of blockchain
technology and a new form of private money – stablecoins – to democratize access
to the dollar. We are now at the second stage of evolution, where the power of
blockchain technology is being used to democratize access to US capital
markets. This is called real world asset (RWA) tokenization. While stablecoins aim

put the US dollar "on chain" as a digital money token, asset market tokenization
could look to put the entirety of US asset markets and their underlying financial

### Page 5

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Deutsche Bank AG Page 5
architecture on chain.
The two forces of tokenized money and tokenized assets are themselves highly
symbiotic. The existence of tokenized assets should widen the appeal of holding
tokenized money like stablecoins, that can then be more easily deployed for returns
and yields "on chain", without needing to be moved off-chain back into fiat currency
to invest in traditional assets. Both stablecoins and tokenized bank deposits could

become important means of payment for tokenized assets, with a meaningful
impact on the settlement process. We will explore the nuances of this in a
forthcoming FX Special Report on Programmable Money.
From a macro perspective, we argue that US tokenization ambitions are deeply
linked to supporting capital inflows into the US. Our earlier research on
stablecoins discussed how they could support the diversification of dollar debt to
private sector holders. Not only did the GENIUS Act introduce 1:1 reserve backing

requirements for dollar stablecoin issuance, that helps power demand for front-end
US T-bills; more importantly, if stablecoins help keep the world paying in dollars,
they help retain the role of the dollar as a private sector savings instrument, which
could help demand for assets like longer-dated Treasuries. If stablecoins are now
combined with a wider universe of tokenized assets, this could encourage demand
for many more US assets. This should help what we have termed a "geopolitics-to-

technology funding swap". The US is moving away from attracting official, long-
term, geopolitically motivated capital flows, towards private sector, shorter-term,
technology motivated capital.
America's full-hearted embrace of asset tokenization – compared with China's
turn away from it – can be understood in the context of both the AI race and the
relative external funding position of both states. If the US is taking a capital-
focused approach to the AI race, with a starting point of twin deficits, the US will

naturally benefit from lowering the hurdles for foreign and private capital as much
as possible. That is indeed what it is doing.
Part 2: America embraces Asset Tokenization
We begin with a definition for asset tokenization, borrowing from the DTCC, the
custodian for close to $115 trillion in US assets and an organization that finds itself
at forefront of this development. Asset tokenization is the "process of converting
ownership rights to a financial asset – such as stocks, bonds or real estate – into

a digital token recorded on a blockchain. These tokens represent the same legal
rights as the original asset but can be transferred, tracked and managed
digitally." They note that tokenization introduces new capabilities, from fractional
ownership, faster cross-border transfers, transparent records and smart contracts.
In a tokenized world, instead of waiting days for a U.S. Treasury bond to settle
through multiple intermediaries, a tokenized version could settle in minutes. Also

see "Asset Tokenization 101" from the DB Research Institute here.
Only around $40bn of real-world assets have been tokenized to date, but the size of
the tokenized asset universe is expected to grow much further and faster from here
given adoption by traditional finance that is just getting underway.
In Part 1 of this paper we explored the macro motivations for America's embrace of
tokenization: to reduce the hurdles for foreign capital to flow into the US and fund

its AI ambitions in an increasingly competitive and existential AI race. In this

### Page 6

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Page 6 Deutsche Bank AG
section, we look at some of the more micro motivations for tokenization, and how
they come together.
Figure 7: Real world tokenized assets have been growing, but are still a fraction
of the over USD100tn US asset universe - leaving big headroom

Some further motivating drivers for asset tokenization
The underlying technology that enables tokenization is distributed ledger
technology (DLT) which allows the ownership rights to anything from money, to a
stock, to a painting, to be represented as a digital token on a decentralized network
like blockchain. But new technology alone cannot drive real world adoption and

change, without the financial and economic incentives to use it.
So, why is DLT being used to tokenize real-world assets today and how have
incentives aligned across economic agents – from retail investors, asset
managers, corporates, financial firms, securities depositories and exchanges –
to motivate this adoption? Beyond the macro, state-level motivation for
attracting capital to fund the AI race and US deficits, we argue that private agents
in the financial system are also likely to be deeply invested for their own reasons.

First, the revolution in overseas retail trading demonstrates the case for widening
access to markets. Retail trading has been spurred on in recent years by a number
of developments: from Covid lockdowns, the rise of zero-commission platforms,
social media, fintech brokerages, improved access to information and charting, and
even tax incentives to increase financialization of household savings (i.e., Japan's
NISA program). Korea is arguably the poster child of the retail revolution, and offers

a potential template for what might become possible at a global scale if technology
is used to lower hurdles for entry. Korea represents 2% of global nominal GDP, but
accounted for 10% of the $740bn of foreign equity inflows to the US last year, a lot
of which were powered by retail investors, or the "seohakaemi" (overseas stock
ants). Access arguably played a big role. Korea began allowing for the fractional
trading of foreign stocks by certain Korean brokerages as early as 2019, even earlier

than for domestic stocks. Korean brokerages also signed up with a number of US
alternative trading systems that offered daytime trading of US stocks in Korean
hours. The combination of a highly digitized, connected population with lower
hurdles for access was a dramatic flow of Korean capital into US equities. To us, the
case of Korea demonstrates what is possible if US markets were to become easily

### Page 7

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Deutsche Bank AG Page 7
accessible 24/7 to everyone.
Second, corporates and institutions still face a number of inefficiencies around
cash and capital in traditional off-chain markets.  We focus on three examples
here: trapped cash for settlement, inefficient collateral management, and manual
corporate actions. While securities markets in the US have successfully moved
from T+2 to T+1, this gap still involves the lock-up of capital to act as a backstop for

trade settlement. If securities and payments were to settle atomically and instantly
on blockchain, this could free up a huge amount of capital. Collateral markets are
also still relatively inflexible today. Today, repo markets allow financial entities to
borrow cash in exchange for lending collateral like US Treasuries. Repos are
structured around overnight to longer-term financing and are constrained by end-
of-day settlement cycles and operational windows. Digital assets held on a

blockchain could arguably be posted instantly for much shorter periods, creating
intraday repo possibilities and increasing the velocity of collateral. As a final
example, NASDAQ highlights that processing corporate actions today alone
"carries costs of more than $58 billion across the industry, driven by fragmented
systems, manual workflows, and duplicative record-keeping.” The
programmability offered by digital assets would help address this as corporate

actions could be programmed into smart contracts attached to the asset. In sum,
there are clear incentives for agents – from corporate issuers to institutional asset
managers – to embrace a tokenized asset environment.
Third, the tokenization train had already left the station but was at risk of being
mediated via third-party offerings  and fragmenting market liquidity. In the
traditional space, we note that fund managers like Blackrock and Templeton had

already begun issuing some of their funds like BUIDL and FBOXX on public
blockchains. This involved making the funds accessible as digital tokens, but
importantly the underlying assets themselves remained off-chain in traditional
financial instruments. More importantly, the presence of blockchain technology
has allowed for entry and innovation by new private players that have begun
offering US assets as digital tokens on blockchain rails. New tokenized equity

frameworks offer representations of real US stocks and ETFs, backed 1:1 by
underlying traditional equities, held in regulated custody, and issued as on-chain
tokens on many different blockchains. They are available to non-US clients in 100+
countries, and allow fractional purchases with as little as $1 in select US stocks and
ETFs, some of which are available to trade 24/7. They do not, however, confer
shareholder rights like voting or dividends in cash – instead, they offer price

exposure to the underlying asset in an always-on format, via a third-party token. If
tokenization was already underway, the incentive for traditional asset mangers and
exchanges to be part of the process was becoming more evident – to avoid losing
funds and liquidity to third parties. Rather than simply tokenizing claims on US
securities, developments underway are now building the infrastructure to allow
underlying US securities themselves to be issued, traded, and held as tokens.

As we discuss in the next section, asset tokenization is not a futuristic or frontier
concept – it is live and is now happening at the heart of traditional markets, led by
core players in the US financial ecosystem.
What is happening?
In a nutshell: tokenization is going mainstream, with adoption by core, traditional
financial players. Securities depositories and exchanges under the aegis of
regulatory guidance are building the market infrastructure to tokenize the US

asset universe, with participation from asset managers and banks.

### Page 8

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Page 8 Deutsche Bank AG
Regulatory efforts arguably kicked off in force with the launch of Project Crypto
by the SEC in July 2025, alongside the White House's Digital Asset Report that
called for laying the groundwork for the US to become the deepest and most liquid
digital asset market in the world. One of the first tasks was to develop a taxonomy
for crypto assets, where tokenized securities were importantly defined as

remaining "securities". In January 2026, Project Crypto evolved into a joint inter-
agency framework between the SEC and the CFTC that would allow for
"coordination, coherence, and a unified approach to the federal oversight of crypto
asset markets". Crucially, it is actions from regulatory agencies in the US that are
driving progress, independent of pending efforts in Congress to pass the CLARITY
Act.
The big green light for mainstream tokenization came in December 2025 when the

SEC issued the DTC with a "no-action" letter which legitimized the idea that the
same security could trade on two rails. DTC could therefore tokenize assets in its
custody from US stocks, ETFs and bonds, and the digital version of these assets
would have "the same entitlements, investor protections and ownership rights" as
the traditional asset. This was followed up by a ruling in January 2026 which noted
that the "format in which a security is issued or the methods by which holders are

recorded (e.g., on-chain vs. off-chain) does not affect application of the federal
securities laws”. Assets can be held traditionally or in tokenized form (as a digital
twin) and they will be treated the same, and will refer to the same security. In other
words, both traditional and tokenized architectures can now coexist, run side-by-
side, and be interoperable, with the trust of DTC custody to back them. While the
passage of the CLARITY Act is still pending, the above SEC notifications and the

commitment of Project Crypto to bringing US capital markets on chain means that
the regulatory umbrella for tokenization has been sufficiently opened. This has been
enabling of developments underway this year.
The progress at DTC remains crucial to track. DTC is the main central securities
depository in the US – it is the venue where trades settle and clear, and the custodian
where financial firms hold their assets. DTC has around $115 trillion of US assets

under its custody. Tokenization at DTC has now begun. In July 2026, the DTC
converted the first asset into tokens that were used for real world trades. 40 different
financial firms participated. Amongst many examples, the SPDR S&P500 ETF Trust
(SPY) – the world's first ETF – was tokenized. In another test, JP Morgan
successfully posted tokenized assets to satisfy a margin requirement at the CME.
The full set of tests that day can be found here. DTC intends to launch its tokenization

service by October 2026, but full-scale tokenization will be a long process. The
DTCC CEO has argued it could proceed over many years with markets "crawling,
then walking, then running" and the pace to be set by market demand to hold assets
as tokens. DTC is beginning by tokenizing the Russell 1000, ETFs, and US Treasuries
before widening it out. Market estimates currently range from $2tn to $30tn for the
size of the tokenized asset universe by the 2030s, but these estimates may well be

revised higher as the infrastructure becomes operational.
US exchanges – NYSE and NASDAQ – are shifting to accommodate "always-on"
trading. In current financial architecture, exchanges are the trading layer, where
buyers and sellers meet and prices are discovered, while DTC is the post-trade
infrastructure layer for settlement and custody, maintaining a central record of
securities ownership after trades occur. The biggest development at US exchanges

is that they are set to move towards round-the-clock trading. NASDAQ has also
announced an intention to offer trading 23/5 trading by December 2025, while
NYSE is aiming for 22 hours by later this year. As noted earlier, synthetic equities

### Page 9

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Deutsche Bank AG Page 9
have already been trading outside market hours on permissionless blockchains and
alternative trading systems, so increasing exchange hours will allow that liquidity
to come back on exchange, and prevent tokenized equities from becoming off-
exchange products that fragment liquidity.
Exchanges are also building new platforms and tools to enable a tokenized
trading experience. The NYSE's new digital platform – being built in conjunction

with Securitize – aims to not just offer 24/7 operations, but also instant settlement,
fractional share trading, and stablecoin based funding. NASDAQ has also
announced the launch of an "equity token design" that will allow public companies
to leverage tokenization to program shareholder engagement from corporate
actions to proxy voting. This will address the current condition of third-party digital
tokens that offer investors economic exposure but without the underlying

shareholder rights and corporate actions. NASDAQ aims to have full tokenization
capabilities by 2027.
The first mover advantage
Tokenization is going mainstream in the US and this is happening now: as we have
described above, the biggest exchanges and securities depositories are making
significant upgrades to allow US asset markets to be traded and held on chain.
While tokenization of the US asset universe could proceed over many years, the ship
has left the harbour.

With the US moving to provide rapid regulatory clarity and support for the
development of tokenized securities, and building the settlement infrastructure
to support it, it stands to benefit from a first mover advantage. Not only might the
US be setting the standards for what on-chain financial trading and settlement
looks like, it could benefit from future network effects. Just as the US benefits from
agency over networks like SWIFT and USD correspondent banking, it may exercise

them over the future on-chain infrastructure. If the majority of on-chain private
money (stablecoins) is dollarized, and the majority of on-chain assets are US
listed, this could reinforce USD payments and capital markets dominance. And if
access to capital is increasingly concentrated in the US, global companies too may
be increasingly interested in listing and issuing in the US to access them. As a
potential harbinger, it is perhaps notable that SK Hynix made the largest foreign

debut in US markets with its $26.5bn ADR this summer, favouring US markets for
their capital raising.
We look at some of the broader implications for markets from the interplay between
the AI race and asset tokenization in Part 3.
See next page

### Page 10

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Page 10 Deutsche Bank AG
Part 3: The future of the dollar, imbalances, and capitalism
In this final section, we look at how the AI race and approach to tokenization could
impact the dollar, and how it might shape the future of global imbalances and
capitalism itself.
Tokenization meets TINA
US equities already account for more than 50% of global equity market
capitalization, and US Treasuries for more than half of OECD sovereign debt. For

many investors, this gives US assets "There is No Alternative" status, popularly
known as TINA: investors have no alternative but to hold assets which are such a
large part of the global market portfolio. The position of US companies in the AI race
thus far has further contributed to TINA, with investors compelled to have exposure
to technology which could change the world. The ultimate outcome of the AI race
will no doubt play an enormous role in whether the US' share of the global market

portfolio grows or shrinks: it is not just how earnings and valuation multiples evolve,
but also how terminal values of the corporate sector could change, and how debt
sustainability could be impacted.
TINA is already playing a big role in attracting capital to the US, but tokenization
could add to this: by making US assets more attractive and more accessible.
First, on attractiveness. The collateral implications of holding US assets on chain
could be very attractive for investors. Fed Governor Cook noted in a speech that the

most significant benefit of tokenization would be implications for capital and
liquidity management for institutions. A tokenized US asset becomes more liquid
and deployable, and capital and cash thus becomes more productive. In simple
terms, you are more likely to hold a tokenized US equity or bond instead of cash, if
you can easily repo your tokenized asset on chain when you need liquidity. If the
value of US assets is going up, the cost of capital for US firms is going down. The

improved collateral velocity of tokenized assets could be another superpower for
US assets, and another arrow in the quiver of US capital attractiveness.
Second, on accessibility. We had argued that stablecoins could give anyone with
an internet connection access to the dollar on chain. Asset tokenization could do the
same for US assets. In a world in which US equities and bonds are trading 24/7,
with instant settlement and much lower costs and frictions, this could have

dramatic knock-on effects on global behaviour, particularly for retail investors
that could find access much eas ier. We find it notable that NASDAQ openly
acknowledged that "the investor base of public equities has the potential to expand
as markets remain open across multiple time zones, making it increasingly
important to have modern tools for public issuers to engage with investors
worldwide.” Tokenization need not also be limited to equities and bonds. One can

imagine a world in which a person with even $1 in a place 10,000 miles away could
own a tiny fraction of a tokenized Times Square on chain.
From an FX perspective, if tokenization succeeds in making US assets more
attractive and accessible, this could help attract private capital flows. It is perhaps
not surprising that the US is leveraging the power of technology – both AI and
blockchain – to attract more foreign private capital at a time when US geopolitical

leadership could be under pressure (see important examples here, here, and here)
and crucially when US fiscal deficits have been widening. Both increase the need
for alternative sources of private sector funding. The US is arguably going "all-in"
on private capital to offset public sector vulnerabilities.

### Page 11

3 September 2026
FX Special Report
Deutsche Bank AG Page 11
But lowering the hurdles for inbound capital, also exposes the US to capital going
out. Tokenization would meaningfully increase capital mobility, in and out of US
assets. The shift in US funding towards private sector, potentially shorter-term
flows into equities – motivated largely by technology returns – will make the dollar
riskier, more equity sensitive, and leveraged to the AI race. We have covered in

earlier research how the dollar's historical properties of diversifying equity risk
could be waning, with the dollar likely to become more positively correlated to risk.
If for instance, AI proves uneconomical or the US starts to fall behind in the AI race,
the dollar could be very exposed.
